Arms of the Maldaathi. (indoors)
A noxious, red-hued fog overwhelms the area with a thick, palpable vapour. This illustrious shop boasts walls of robust granite that stand as a testament to Strength and the Truths. Beneath, a darkwood floor is adorned with a meticulously crafted rug depicting intricate battle scenes, a masterpiece woven with threads that seem to tell tales of valour and conquest. Artfully placed around the shop, mannequins donned in gleaming armour catch the ambient light, creating a captivating display of martial prowess. Each piece, meticulously crafted, reflects the expert artisanship that defines the Maldaathi knights. Iron sconces, housing torches that flicker with an unwavering flame, cast a warm and inviting glow upon the space. One wall, adorned with a discreet inscription in Qith from the Apocrypha, whispers one of the Truths in subtle silver letters. Preserving the rich history of the Maldaathi knights, the proud guild flag hangs regally, a symbol of their importance within the city of Mhaldor and a constant reminder of the honour and steadfast courage that echoes through the ages. Shunai Bridge. (road)
The rumble of burdened caravan wheels carries across the area. Nature's fecundity floods the surrounds with the clamour of life in a chittering refrain. The stars glimmer down, broken only upon occasion by thin wisps of clouds. A marvel of engineering second only to the road which it supports, the great Shunai Bridge crosses the restless waters of the Urubamba, providing safe passage for all travellers. Made of massive stone pylons and rare, nearly indestructible woods, only those master builders, Dwarves, could have crafted such a structure. The view from this moderate elevation shows the river cutting south through marsh and grass before it curves in the distance to water portions of the Black Forest and more remote places. Somewhere far to the north, past any hope of seeing, is the river's source, the humble beginnings of one of the great rivers that flow across Sapience. A logosmas stocking has been hung here, awaiting gifts. A sigil in the shape of a small, rectangular monolith is on the ground. A vicious death adder is here, hood flared in warning. There are 2 black-crowned nycticoraxes here. Staring about himself with an eternal frown, a spectral gorilla broods here. The twisted remains of Xylarys lie here. Gavai, the Jamala is here, hidden. His features are obscured by Revelation's Guise. He wields Elentari's Scourge in his left hand and a Shield of Absorption in his right. Slave Morasla, Pestilent Disciple is here, hidden. Her features are obscured by a darkened mask dripping with gold. She wields Adchachel's lament in her right hand. Steward Layta Lichlord, Red Reaper is here, hidden. Her features are obscured by an unsettling porcelain doll-face mask. She wields Agith'maal's ire in her left hand and a bone shield reinforced with blackened steel in her right. Truthseeker Vasyly is here. He wields Reaver, a dark brass morningstar in his left hand and Sentinel, a darkened-silver morningstar in his right. Eradicator Maldrantus is here, hidden. His features are obscured by a mask of lifevision. He wields a Shield of Absorption in his left hand and Agith'maal's ire in his right. Tyndran Tabethys Aristata, Tyrannical Butcher of Baelgrim is riding on a silvery spectral gorilla. She wields an exquisite Mhaldorian staff of sterling silver in her left hand and a Shield of Absorption in her right. Valkyri, Seeker of Elocution is here. She wields a Braincrusher flail in each hand. Wreathed in torrid fury, the Elemental Lord Agathon blazes bright. He wields a Shield of Absorption in his left hand. Tomekeeper Harkon d'Ivoire, Scourge of His Legion is here. He wields a leaf-bladed ritual knife in his left hand.
You see exits leading east and west.

Old road nearing plains. (road)
The rumble of burdened caravan wheels carries across the area. Nature's fecundity floods the surrounds with the clamour of life in a chittering refrain. The stars glimmer down, broken only upon occasion by thin wisps of clouds. A rock-lined road meets a dirt track here, transitioning as it reaches farther in the wilderness of Sapience. The forest has faded away, the green ferns and bushes giving way to the unadulterated grasses of the plains. The flatter landscape of this Savannah starkly showcases a distant boulder, marring the rolling horizon with its looming shape. The nearby dwelling seems similarly out of place, a lone fortress of civilisation that has already fallen victim to decay. Long and double-storied, it seems to have once been worthy of the label mansion, but faded paint, broken windows, and a battered door reduce its splendour. Fresh new ruts are pressed deeply into the ground here. Turgid and blistered, a severed leg lies motionless here. A large cart rests here, stacked high with assorted containers secured with thick rope. There are 2 black-crowned nycticoraxes here. Slave Morasla, Pestilent Disciple is here, hidden. Her features are obscured by a darkened mask dripping with gold. She wields Adchachel's lament in her right hand. Steward Layta Lichlord, Red Reaper is here, hidden. Her features are obscured by an unsettling porcelain doll-face mask. She wields Agith'maal's ire in her left hand and a bone shield reinforced with blackened steel in her right. Truthseeker Vasyly is here. He wields Reaver, a dark brass morningstar in his left hand and Sentinel, a darkened-silver morningstar in his right. Eradicator Maldrantus is here, hidden. His features are obscured by a mask of lifevision. He wields a Shield of Absorption in his left hand and Agith'maal's ire in his right. Valkyri, Seeker of Elocution is here. She wields a Braincrusher flail in each hand. Wreathed in torrid fury, the Elemental Lord Agathon blazes bright. He wields a Shield of Absorption in his left hand. Tomekeeper Harkon d'Ivoire, Scourge of His Legion is here. He wields a leaf-bladed ritual knife in his left hand.
You see exits leading northeast, south, and west (open door).

Path over mountains. (road)
The rumble of burdened caravan wheels carries across the area. A noxious, red-hued fog overwhelms the area with a thick, palpable vapour. The stars glimmer down from a clear and cloudless sky, bathing the landscape in silvery light. Precarious and serpentine, this path runs around the circumference of the base of the mountain, winding slowly into the lofty heights where the air is crisp and rarified. Though the elevation is still relatively low compared to the mountain tops, there is a clear drop in temperature as the cold northern air rushes from the mountains. A large cart rests here, stacked high with assorted containers secured with thick rope. There are 2 black-crowned nycticoraxes here. Slave Morasla, Pestilent Disciple is here, hidden. Her features are obscured by a darkened mask dripping with gold. She wields Adchachel's lament in her right hand. Steward Layta Lichlord, Red Reaper is here, hidden. Her features are obscured by an unsettling porcelain doll-face mask. She wields Agith'maal's ire in her left hand and a bone shield reinforced with blackened steel in her right. Truthseeker Vasyly is here. He wields Reaver, a dark brass morningstar in his left hand and Sentinel, a darkened-silver morningstar in his right. Eradicator Maldrantus is here, hidden. His features are obscured by a mask of lifevision. He wields a Shield of Absorption in his left hand and Agith'maal's ire in his right. Valkyri, Seeker of Elocution is here. She wields a Braincrusher flail in each hand. Wreathed in torrid fury, the Elemental Lord Agathon blazes bright. He wields a Shield of Absorption in his left hand. Tomekeeper Harkon d'Ivoire, Scourge of His Legion is here. He wields a leaf-bladed ritual knife in his left hand.
You see exits leading up and down.

A pass over the mountains. (road)
The rumble of burdened caravan wheels carries across the area. A noxious, red-hued fog overwhelms the area with a thick, palpable vapour. A poisonous black mist blankets the ground. Sand has covered the location. The stars glimmer down from a clear and cloudless sky, bathing the landscape in silvery light. A noxious, red-hued fog overwhelms the area with a thick, palpable vapour. Sand has covered the location. The stars glimmer down from a clear and cloudless sky, bathing the landscape in silvery light. As you climb among the canyon and rising mountain peaks, the roar of a waterfall becomes faintly audible, adding to the melody of the howling wind among these rocky heights. Passing the Black Forest. (road)
The rumble of burdened caravan wheels carries across the area. Nature's fecundity floods the surrounds with the clamour of life in a chittering refrain. Pale radiance bathes the land, a subtle ripple in the celestial vault forming into a faded moonbow. The forest hovers a few steps beyond the road, gradually expanding on both sides to make this segment darker through leafy shade. The distinctive scent of pine and the more faint perfume of musty leaves and loam mix within the air as it whirls through this narrow jaunt. Leaves are scattered across the road along with rocks and the occasional bird, briefly daring out of the Black Forest to search for discarded food or sparkling treasures. Quite innocently, a banana peel lies on the ground. Staring about himself with an eternal frown, a spectral gorilla broods here. Tyndran Tabethys Aristata, Tyrannical Butcher of Baelgrim is riding on a silvery spectral gorilla. She wields a Shield of Absorption in her left hand and an exquisite Mhaldorian staff of sterling silver in her right. Wreathed in torrid fury, the Elemental Lord Agathon blazes bright. He wields a Shield of Absorption in his left hand.
You see exits leading northeast, south, and northwest.
